18. Risk register

RiskLikelihoodMitigation
iPhone readback or 3D compositing makes 30 fps capture too costlymediumFour paths measured in Phase 0; the runtime governor keeps the 10% budget; the worst case is a lower capture rate on old phones, never a slower game.
Retro Arcade already crashes on iPhone (EmulatorJS #1143)high#28i; memory options tested in PR 3.4; the Record-only state if the soak fails; a weekly watch for an upstream fix.
Browser updates change encoder behaviormediumProbes at every arm, the TTFC watchdog, weekly local runs.
Kids lose clips to Safari evictionmediumSave to Photos coaching, the Home Screen nudge, the honest eviction notice, My links from the server (Phase 7), Keep in my account (decision 6).
A name reaches a clip through a later kid editmediumThe scrub hash gate turns clips off on any edit after the scrub; the input-to-canvas source scan; the literal scan; screening for hosted links.
Hosted links become an abuse vectormediumScreening including game-likeness, reserved pools, fair queue, hide-and-restore reports, fail-closed attestation, session-blind until #26i.
Phase 1 behavior changes surprise kidsmediumDecision 3 sets speeds; per-game speed tests; live smoke after each PR.
Program size causes review sprawlmedium2-3 modules per integration PR, split engine PRs, the /dcr convergence rule.
Licensing (FFmpeg LGPL in WASM, mediabunny MPL-2.0, server-side H.264 transcode)lowSeparate .wasm files with source and build script, a credits page, no GPL flags in shipped WASM, no H.264 encoder distributed.

19. Decisions (made by Claude under Jack's delegation, 2026-09-28)

Jack, 2026-09-28: "you make choices based on makign a fucking great gameplay system dont make me make technical choices or whatever you get it all working great and think outside the box if needed". Every item below is decided. Jack is asked only for facts or actions that legally or physically need him (end of this section).

#DecisionWhy
1Build hosted links and Send to a phone (Phase 7, behind its flag and the legal checklist).Links are the only way to get a clip to a friend who has no share-sheet app, to a desktop kid's phone, and into iMessage as an autoplaying preview that brings friends back. The cost stays in single-digit to tens of dollars a month with hard ceilings.
2Remove the gory titles from Retro Arcade (Mortal Kombat 1-3, Doom, Killer Instinct, Wolfenstein 3D, Alien 3, Alien vs. Predator), closing #25i.Guardrail 1 is explicit: no blood or gore for kids 6-14. Removing them is cleaner than a gate kids would learn to click through, and it also shrinks the clip allowlist problem.
3Keep today's game speed for Blitz Bomber, Dino Runner, Endless Runner and Platformer (a 0.5 time scale inside the shared loop).Kids learned these games at this speed and every stored best was earned at it. The bug being fixed is the frame-rate dependence (double speed on 120 Hz, dead keyboard), not the feel.
4Brag surfaces: (a) yes, "Watch the run" beside leaderboard scores for the board and tile games; (b) yes, the same for live games once links exist; (c) no public gallery; (d) yes, owner-only emoji reactions on link pages.Watching the run behind a top score is the most Steam-like brag there is, and move logs make it nearly free. A gallery is a feed that needs heavy moderation and invites strangers.
5Link life: established 30 days, renewable to 90; unestablished 7 days.Long enough for a group chat to enjoy it, short enough to keep storage and exposure small.
6Yes to "Keep in my account" (private cloud copies of kept clips), after #26i.Clips should follow a kid to a new phone and survive Safari's 7-day wipe. The cost is fractions of a cent per kid.
7Yes to anonymous field health counters, stated in the privacy notice.No lab holds every device; without them a broken phone model stays invisible.
8No email for kid accounts (#26i). Username plus a picture password (pick three pictures, the way kids' reading apps do it), plus a printable sign-in card with a QR code that signs you in on a new device and doubles as a grown-up's recovery key. Existing email accounts migrate at their next sign-in, and the email is deleted. PR 7.0 researches and builds the details.This removes the COPPA problem at the root, fits kids who cannot type or remember passwords, and the sign-in card solves moving to a new phone.
9Devices: the iPhone SE 3rd gen (iOS 27) is the reference phone. The Samsung SM-X230 is used after the Home Assistant session is done with it. Rows with no device are marked "blocked on hardware".Jack's answer, 2026-09-28.
10Shirt text in both Four-Wheelers becomes preset words, and "HANK" stays one of the presets.Typed text drawn into the game picture is the one way a real name leaks into a shared clip. Presets keep the fun, and a first name is allowed by guardrail 7.
11Emoji paired with a word on nav links and chips; headings stay plain text.Pre-readers navigate by picture. Pairing avoids the "emoji as decoration" slop tell.
12Keep "HANK'S GARAGE" and "HANK'S HOUSE" in the Four-Wheeler worlds.It is Hank's world, and guardrail 7 allows a first name in public games.
13Moderation numbers: 48 h review time, 24 h auto-restore for low-reputation reports, 7 days to count as established, and the pool sizes in 13.2.These are defaults in one settings row, tuned from real volume.
14New: "Beat my score" challenges. A clip or link carries the score as a challenge. Opening it (the link page, or a clip shared with its link) starts the game with a ghost target ("Beat 12,400!") shown in the HUD and a celebration when it falls. Local in Phase 5, through links in Phase 7.Thinking outside the box: bragging is better when the friend can answer it. It needs no new storage and turns every shared clip into a reason to play.
15New: build order by game group. Phase 1 first lands the shared primitives (audio bus, game loop, input guards, result chip, header) with no game changes. Each later PR then takes a group of 2-3 games through every class fix and its clip integration at once.Jack merges by hand. Per-class PRs across all games would all touch the same files and collide. Per-group PRs touch disjoint files, so they can be built in parallel and merged in any order.
16As built: the engine ships as one PR (#43pr: contract, capture, encode worker, io worker), and every Wave A PR is rebased onto the platform PR (#37pr).The three engine parts are dead code until the clip service imports them, so one merge carries no user risk, and it removes a four-way merge order. Branches that share their base commits can merge in any order, and each one gates on the same platform.
17Our own AAC WASM build from a pinned, hash-verified FFmpeg release in a pinned Emscripten image, with the exact source tarball hosted under /licenses (PR 0.5).@mediabunny/aac-encoder statically links FFmpeg's LGPL-2.1 libraries, and upstream does not record the FFmpeg revision, so the "complete corresponding source" that LGPL section 6 requires cannot be given for it. A reproducible build of a known release can. SIMD only: the support floor (Safari 16.4, Chrome 111, Firefox 111) has WASM SIMD, so no scalar build is needed.
18Tiers M and V get their MediaRecorder engine in Wave C (rotating recorders, WebM in the library), not later.Without it, Firefox Android, older Firefox desktop and 32-bit Android Chrome show no clip button at all. The service leaves an engine seam so the recorder plugs in behind the same contract.
19Retention: an account nobody uses for 24 months is deleted, with everything it owns. Parents get an answer within 30 days, a parent is verified through the account email, and hand-made database copies are deleted within 90 days (#42pr).16 CFR 312.10 forbids keeping children's data indefinitely and names "a specific amount of time after the child has last used" the service. 24 months matches ABCmouse, Kiddopia and Legends of Learning. Production read 2026-09-28: 4 accounts, none eligible before 2027-12-28.
20Test gates survive machine load: a suite-wide 60 s vitest timeout (no per-test timeouts) and a timer guard that clears component timers before jsdom goes away (#37pr).This Mac runs several agent sessions at once, and at a load average of 60-70 healthy tests timed out or a leaked 100 ms timer failed the whole run. A gate that fails on load gets bypassed; one that does not stays trusted.
21No code change for the client IP (#29i closed).A live probe sent 125 forged addresses through both Cloudflare and the raw Railway domain; all landed in one rate-limit bucket, and Cloudflare rejects a client-sent CF-Connecting-IP. PR 0.4 is therefore done without a diff.
22A cancelled press never makes a clip, whatever its length.Two review fixes gave "cancelled" two meanings, and the result chip then made a clip that nobody asked for. One meaning removes the ambiguity, and every UI path that lets a press go depends on it.
23Drag off more than 48 px to cancel a clip-button press.This is how a native iOS button works. The 48 px margin keeps the tap of a small, unsteady finger.
24The result chip clips only this run: "Watch the whole run" for a run of 30 s or less, else "Watch the end" and "Make the whole run a video". Record and Take a picture leave the chip.A fixed 30 s clip reached back into the previous run after a restart. At game over, Record and Take a picture had no use and put 7 buttons on a 320 px screen.
25A clip's poster is the featured moment, else the last keyframe at least 1 s before the end.The first keyframe was the dim start of the run, so every tile of a game looked the same.
26No clip button, no capture. If the clip UI cannot load, the engine stays parked and the load is tried again after 2 s, 10 s, 60 s and on each return to the page.Capture with no button spends the battery and gives the kid nothing.
27EmulatorJS 4.2.3 is served from this site with a SHA-256 manifest; its CDN is blocked by CSP, and the page accepts only the 7 arcade systems (#38pr).A third-party script in the origin that holds the clip library could read every clip on the device. A missing core also fell back to the CDN, and ?core= could load any core.
28The emulator source is served from the same place, fetched by hash in the Docker build, never stored in git.GPL-2.0 section 3, GPL-3.0 section 6(d) and MPL-2.0 section 3.2 require the source next to the object code. The 19 archives are 136 MB, which git would keep in every clone forever.
29Game Boy runs on mGBA, not gambatte.gambatte is GPL-2.0-only and upstream links it with GPL-3.0 RetroArch. mGBA (MPL-2.0) passed the acid2 tests pixel-exact, input and audio, and old battery saves move over. Known residual: upstream links stella2014, snes9x, genesis_plus_gx and picodrive the same way. We ship them unmodified with every license text, the full source and a NOTICE; removing them removes Atari 2600, SNES and Genesis.
30Retro Arcade save states stay on the device (IndexedDB Blobs, one set per account) and are never synced to the cloud.The old code saved an empty string, so every save state was lost. States are 0.2-16 MB, over the 1 MB cloud slot, and cloud copies would add server cost.

What only Jack can provide (asked once, batched)

  • Before PR 0.3 ships: a mailing address that is not your home (a PO box or a business address), a phone number that is not personal (a free Google Voice number works), and a contact email, for the privacy notice. COPPA requires all three on the site.
  • Before Phase 7 launches: file the Copyright Office PO-box waiver, then register the DMCA agent ($6) and the NCMEC ESP under your name. I prepare every form.
  • Production writes waiting for a grant: (1) cancelled 2026-10-02: no ROM object is ever deleted from the hanks-hits-roms bucket (Jack: the ROMs are his library). Content rules act only on the retro-arcade catalog and the /api/roms proxy; Cloudflare held no cached copy of a blocked ROM; (2) done 2026-10-02: railway config apply for the Railway IaC migration, and railway.toml deleted after it (.railway/README.md); (3) done after the apply: delete the unused Railway variable NEXT_PUBLIC_ROM_CDN_URL (the build takes /api/roms from apps/web/.env.production; a Railway value never reached the build, and if it were ever wired up the new emulator CSP would refuse its ROMs); (4) done 2026-10-01: CLIPS_MODE is on.
  • Ongoing: merge PRs; grant the production writes as each is named; moderation alerts once links exist.

20. What the reviews changed

Wave 1 (six lenses, 125 findings: 5 critical, 73 major, 47 minor) produced rev 2.

Wave 2 (traceability and consistency, 58 findings: 2 critical, 23 major, 33 minor) found 34 wave-1 items incomplete and several contradictions rev 2 introduced. Rev 3 fixes all of them. The critical ones:

  • The session timeline and Record now roll one journal part per encoder epoch and join parts by the same rules as clips (6.6, 8.3).
  • Four-Wheeler Adventure's typed shirt word, drawn into the captured canvas, is now in section 10, PR 1.6 and the source scan.

The major ones:

  • A content-hash scrub gate that trips on any later edit.
  • One header slot in every state, with designed widths.
  • Phrase-level copy rules with "save" reserved for copies that leave the site.
  • Jack's full action table, including the dogfood writes and the decision-blocked PRs.
  • A cost table with stated screener concurrency, caps and every screening stage.
  • A school fallback on its own authenticated route.
  • A tap that always means "clip" during play, with a separate new-clip chip.
  • Hold-to-menu that commits nothing.
  • Real pauses for the four open-world games.
  • A complete state machine with a button look per state.
  • Scene-text exemptions for our own painted text.
  • Two link lifetimes driven by the real expiry.
  • Split hold retention.
  • Offline-safe flags and a lock that only runs when configured.
  • A production default that flips on at release.
  • PR 7.0 for #26i.
  • Phase 4 re-sized.
  • Leaderboard reachable at game over.
  • Full header sets on /c/*.
  • Numbers for every former placeholder.

Findings: design/clips/research/plan-review-2026-09-27.json (wave 1) and plan-review-wave2-2026-09-27.json.

Review loop status: wave 2 still found defects that rev 2 introduced, so by the /dcr rule the next review happens per PR during the build, not as a third plan wave. Phase 0's measured memo is the next planned revision of this document.

Appendix A: per-game integration specs

Rules in sections 1-19 override Appendix A wherever they differ. The appendix is generated from the research specs and predates both reviews. Known conflicts, resolved in favor of the body:

  • No gamer tag and no site name in any live or replay frame. Drop any handle chip the appendix paints.
  • Capture fps comes from each registration's fps target plus the rung table in 6.2. "30 to 24" notes are replaced by that table.
  • Audio anchors use production time (6.4), never getOutputTimestamp, and every tap connects through a zero-gain sink.
  • Retro Arcade audio uses the constructor shim only, never EmulatorJS internals such as AL.currentCtx.
  • Prerequisite fixes in a Phase 1 class land in Phase 1: loops, input (including Monster Truck pedals, #18i), restart grace, new best (including Monster Truck records and Four-Wheeler Adventure race bests), sound (including Monster Truck engine and music), read-aloud and hi-DPI.
  • Oregon Trail is entirely Phase 4 (PR 4.6).
  • Four-Wheeler Adventure's shirt text becomes presets in PR 1.6.

Re-read every file:line before editing (read at repo HEAD 20d56b1 on 2026-09-27).

Trivia, Drum Machine and Virtual Pet specs are written in PR 4.7.

Phase 2: canvas games

arkanoid canvas 17 h

Prerequisite fixes

  • Data bug: highestMultiplier never rises above 1. The loop calls updateMultiplier(0) at Game.tsx:343 immediately before endGame at :347, so store.ts:160-162 compares a multiplier that is already 1. The profile's 'Best Multiplier' (gameStatExtractor.ts:414) is therefore always 1x. Fix: track runPeakMultiplier in updateMultiplier and use it in endGame.
  • The dead 🔊 toggle (#24i): add real SFX through the shared bus, with voice caps, and wire the toggle to the speaker mute.
  • Resize blanks the arena. The resize handler (Game.tsx:371-375) clears the canvas, and nothing repaints outside 'playing', so rotating on the result or pause screen leaves a blank arena under the card and in the clip. Fix: after setting width/height, call render(ctx, canvas, useArkanoidStore.getState().balls) when a 2D context exists. This is a one-line fix.
  • Perf headroom (gate: recording at 60 fps must hold p95 frame time under 16.7 ms with 100+ balls on a mid Android and an older iPhone): - Pre-render the static checkerboard and walls into an offscreen canvas on resize. At DPR 3, render :132-154 issues about 1,500 fillRect calls per frame. - Replace the selector-less useArkanoidStore() (:14-31) with narrow selectors, so React stops re-rendering the tree every frame. - If the gate still fails, enforce the documented but never-read GAME.maxBalls=150 (constants.ts:94) in addBall (store.ts:180).

Registration

File apps/web/src/games/arkanoid/Game.tsx. Insert the hook after the forceSync effect (after :50) and before the pointer effect at :52: useClipSource('arkanoid', { canvas: () => canvasRef.current /* ref :11, <canvas> :420-426 */, phase: () => ({menu:'menu', playing:'playing', paused:'paused', gameOver:'result'} as const)[useArkanoidStore.getState().gameState], layout: { preset: 'fit-source' /* portrait 720x1280 when canvas.height > canvas.width, else landscape 1280x720 */, bandPx: {portrait:176, landscape:96}, letterbox: '#1e293b' }, fps: 60, hud: () => { const s = useArkanoidStore.getState(); return { score: s.score, best: s.progress.highScore, extras: [`${s.multiplier}x`] }; }, paintHud: paintArkanoidHud, paintResult: paintArkanoidResult }). Put the painters in a new island file, games/arkanoid/lib/clipPainters.ts. They read useArkanoidStore.getState() on every tick and never read the DOM. Lifecycle facts the hook must handle: - The canvas never unmounts or remounts. There is no key, and the menu and game-over overlays are siblings at :462 and :484. - The GameShell restart calls startGame (ArkanoidGameShell.tsx:29), and startGame emits runStart, which the kit treats as a cut. - The resize effect (:367-380) rewrites canvas.width/height on rotation. The kit must re-fit on every tick and insert a cut when the source dimensions change. - ArkanoidGame re-renders every frame: it calls useArkanoidStore() with no selector (:14-31), and updateBalls runs every frame (:229, :342). So useClipSource must keep its config in a ref and register once per canvas element, never on each render. - The loop effect (:198-364) has stable dependencies, so it needs no loop migration. The kit samples the canvas in its own rAF, or through onAfterRender if the loop later moves to useGameLoop.

What the compositor paints

The kit paints the HUD band and the result card. Arkanoid's canvas paints only the grid, walls, paddle and balls (:114-195), so a raw capture has no numbers at all. (1) HUD band (paintArkanoidHud), above the game rect, copying the DOM strip at :394-406: - Background #0f172a (bg-slate-900, :391). - Score: bold white, centered, about 72 px in the portrait band (text-3xl/md:text-4xl), formatted with score.toLocaleString() like the DOM. - Sub-line in #94a3b8: `${multiplier}x Multiplier • High: ${progress.highScore.toLocaleString()}`. - Leave out the sound button (:409-415) and the launch hint pill (:429-435). They are UI chrome, not gameplay. (2) Ball-counter chips on the game rect's top-left, copying :438-458: - Draw them only when gameState==='playing' and the count is above 0. - Chip: rgba(30,41,59,0.8), radius 8. - Dot colors: #3b82f6 blue (h-6), #f97316 orange (h-6), #facc15 yellow (h-4). - Label: bold white `x${n}`. - Counts are balls.filter(b => b.type === ...) over getState().balls. (3) Result card (paintArkanoidResult, phase 'result'), copying the DOM overlay at :484-502: - Fill the game rect with rgba(15,23,42,0.95). - 'Game Over!' in bold white. - `Score: ${score.toLocaleString()}` in #facc15. - '🎉 New High Score! 🎉' in #4ade80 when wasNewHighScore is true. - `High Score: ${progress.highScore.toLocaleString()}` in #94a3b8. - No Play Again button. The loop returns before rendering on loss (:346-349). So the frame under the card is the last gameplay frame, which the kit re-draws. Emoji use the platform font, the same as the DOM.

Clip layout

The layout follows the source aspect, which is device-dependent because the arena is stretched to the viewport. Portrait phones: 720x1280. - HUD band: 176 px. - Game rect: the canvas fitted into 720x1104, letterboxed with #1e293b (GAME.canvasColor, constants.ts:95) so the bars read as arena. Desktop landscape: 1280x720. - HUD band: 96 px. - Game rect: fitted into 1280x624. Scaling: - The source is already viewport x devicePixelRatio (about 1170x2178 on a 3x iPhone, :371-375), so the kit always downsamples. - Use imageSmoothingEnabled=true and imageSmoothingQuality='high'. The content is smooth vector art (radial gradients), not pixel art. - Do not change the backing store. Frame rate: 60 fps preferred (fast multi-ball motion), with the kit's adaptive drop to 30. This needs an H.264 Level 4.0 codec string (see groupNotes).

Brag events

Use the shared createRunBrag('arkanoid') helper from the store (lib/store.ts), so the edge detection lives in one place. - runStart: in startGame (store.ts:99-116), call brag.start(progress.highScore) and reset runPeakMultiplier to 1. - newHighScore (highlight): in addScore (:211-215), after computing the new score, call brag.score(newScore). It fires once per run, on the first moment newScore > bestAtRunStart, and only when bestAtRunStart > 0. First-ever runs stay silent, matching achievements/evaluate.ts:115-121. The payload is {prevBest: bestAtRunStart, newBest, runId}. The comparison is against the snapshot taken at runStart, never progress.highScore, which endGame bumps. - combo, rising edge only: updateMultiplier (:217-224) runs every frame (Game.tsx:343), and ball counts flap around 10/20/50. So emit {type:'combo', tier} only when mult > runPeakMultiplier, then set runPeakMultiplier = mult. Each tier fires at most once per run. 5x and 10x are highlights; 2x is a marker. - runEnd: in endGame (:150-171), call const isNewBest = brag.end(state.score). end() uses a strict greater-than, so a tie is not a new best. Set wasNewHighScore = isNewBest, then emit {type:'runEnd', score, prevBest, isNewBest}. The existing highScore update at :156-158 stays. - setProgress (:233-235) during an active run: call brag.raiseBest(data.highScore). A cloud sync that lands mid-run with a higher server best then cannot produce a false new best. - Pause and resume emit nothing. The phase getter covers them.

Audio

Arkanoid is silent today. There is no AudioContext anywhere in the island, and the 🔊 toggle (Game.tsx:409-415, store.ts:227-229, persisted at :241) controls nothing (#24i). Add games/arkanoid/lib/sounds.ts on the shared bus. Every voice connects to gameAudio.out('arkanoid'), never ctx.destination. Triggers: - Launch whoosh: in launchBall (store.ts:120-136). This call happens inside the tap gesture. - Wall tick: in the Game.tsx loop where hitWall=true (:276). Pitch varies by ball type. Cap it with gameAudio.voice('arkanoid','wall',{maxPerSec:12}). - Paddle boing: at the paddle bounce (:294). - Spawn pop: at addBall (:310-317). Pitch goes up with the live ball count, and the pop gets brighter for orange and yellow-dot balls. Cap: 16 per second. - Tier-up arpeggio: on the combo rising edge (2x, 5x, 10x get rising 3, 4 and 5 note runs). - Last-ball-lost thud, then a game-over sting, in endGame. - New-best fanfare: when isNewBest is true. The caps are required. A chain reaction with 50 to 150 balls produces dozens of wall hits and spawns per second, and uncapped oscillators would clip the mix and the recording. Mute: toggleSound and hydration call gameAudio.setSpeakerMuted('arkanoid', !soundEnabled). Voices are always synthesized into the bus, and the mute gates only the speaker path, so clips keep game sound. The editor's own 'Game sounds' switch decides what goes into the file. This makes the existing toggle real and closes #24i.

Brag tests (store-level, games/arkanoid/__tests__/brag.test.ts): - newHighScore fires once when the score crosses a prior best of 500, and never on a first run with best 0. - A tie is not a new best. - Combo fires once per tier even when updateMultiplier flaps between 9 and 10 balls for 100 calls. - raiseBest suppresses a false best after a mid-run setProgress. - highestMultiplier records the run peak (10), not 1. Audio test (games/arkanoid/__tests__/audio.test.ts), with a mocked AudioContext and bus: - Voices connect to the bus input, never to destination. - 200 wall hits within 1 s produce at most 12 voices. - The mute sets the speaker gain, and voices still reach the record tap. The existing tests in __tests__/Game.test.tsx and store.test.ts stay green. asteroids canvas 12 h

Prerequisite fixes

  • Add a new-high-score flag and paint it. gameOver (store.ts:307-319) sets no flag, and neither the canvas nor the DOM ever says 'NEW HIGH SCORE'. Compute it as score > bestAtRunStart and draw 'NEW HIGH SCORE!' in the in-canvas game-over block (Game.tsx:199-215, for example at y=H/2+75 in #FFD700).
  • The wave-complete jingle is mistimed (store.ts:289 fires on the tap-to-continue), and the final death plays the sound twice (:611/:661 plus :309). Move and split them as described in the audio field.
  • Accidental restart. A kid holding Space to fire gets OS key auto-repeat, and at gameOver the next repeat instantly calls startGame (Game.tsx:315-321). Ignore e.repeat for restart and advance, and lock canvas-tap and Space restarts for 600 ms after gameOver or waveComplete (:315-329, :435-443). The result card stays readable and the kit's share chip stays reachable.
  • Replace the per-frame re-created rAF effect (Game.tsx:264-280) with the shared useGameLoop at a fixed 60 Hz step. This removes the per-frame rAF re-registration and fixes 2x game speed on 120 Hz displays. Say so in the PR: it restores the designed speed for 120 Hz players.
  • Hi-res backing store (S=1.92) so HUD text and 2 px lines are crisp in the clip, and on retina phones, where the 500 px store is currently CSS-upscaled.

Registration

The phase getter reads store.status, so recording pauses for both. - New runs start from the GameShell restart (AsteroidsGameShell.tsx:32), from a canvas click at gameOver (:436-437), and from Space at gameOver (:315-321). What the compositor paints

No overlay painter is needed. The canvas paints its own HUD: 'Score:' and 'Wave:' (:148-155) and ship-glyph lives (:157-173). It also paints the PAUSED card (:185-197, never recorded because the phase is paused), the GAME OVER card (:199-215) and 'WAVE n COMPLETE!' (:217-229). The game loop stops outside 'playing', and the status effect paints the result or interstitial once. So in those phases the kit repeats the last source frame, and it freezes that frame when the phase exits (the kit's result-freeze rule). The DOM stats bar (:455-462) and the start card (:484-501) are not captured. They are not needed. The only drawing addition is the prerequisite in-canvas 'NEW HIGH SCORE!' line on the game-over block, so the brag appears inside the frame.

Clip layout

Square 960x960 (921,600 px, the 720p pixel budget exactly; both sides are multiples of 16). - Source: the 1.92x backing store (960x960), drawn 1:1, so no resampling is needed and the thin 2 px vector strokes and 2 px bullets stay sharp. - Smoothing: enabled (vector art). - Editor 'story' variant: 720x1280 with the game at 720x720 centered (y=280). Title and score come from hud(), and the brand goes in the bands, colored #0a0a0a (COLORS.BACKGROUND, constants.ts). - Frame rate: 60 fps.

Brag events

All events come from the store, lib/store.ts, through createRunBrag('asteroids'). - runStart: in startGame (:260-271), call brag.start(progress.highScore). - newHighScore (highlight, once per run, prevBest > 0 only): call brag.score(score) once per update, just before each set that commits a score: the normal commit (:744), the wave-complete commit (:724) and the two final-life commits (:630, :681). Score rises at :518 (asteroid) and :578 (UFO). - levelClear (highlight): at the waveComplete transition (:723-741), emit {type:'levelClear', level: wave}. - UFO destroyed (:573-603): emit {type:'bonus', label:'UFO'} as a marker. - runEnd: in gameOver (:307-319), call const isNewBest = brag.end(state.score) and store it in a new state field isNewHighScore. startGame resets it. Then emit runEnd. - Every death path funnels through gameOver: the hyperspace death at :328-332, the asteroid collision at :629-647 and the UFO bullet at :680-698. No other emitter is needed. - Edge: gameOver today only runs Math.max (:315) and sets no flag, so the previous best must come from the runStart snapshot, never from progress.

Audio

Existing sound code: - Private lazy context: store.ts:176-184. - playSound: :186-252, wiring oscillator -> gain -> ctx.destination at :194-195. - Call sites: :289 wave (in nextWave), :309 death (in gameOver), :325 hyperspace, :427 shoot, :566 explode, :599 explode (UFO), :611 and :661 death. Migration: - Delete getAudioContext. playSound gets its context from gameAudio.context() and ends in gainNode.connect(gameAudio.out('asteroids')). - The `enabled` early return becomes gameAudio.setSpeakerMuted('asteroids', !progress.soundEnabled). Wire it in the toggle (Game.tsx:445-448) and on hydrate, so clips keep sound. Fixes and additions: (a) The wave jingle plays in nextWave (:289), that is, after the kid taps. The recorded wave-complete moment is silent. Play it at the waveComplete transition (:723) instead, and give nextWave a short 'launch' blip. (b) The final death plays 'death' twice: the collision path at :611/:661 plays it, then gameOver at :309 plays it again. Give gameOver a distinct game-over sting. (c) 'thrust' exists in the type (:186) but is never played. Add a sustained thrust rumble (lowpass-filtered noise, gain about 0.05). Start and stop it on the thrusting input edges in setInput (:350-352), and stop it on pause and gameOver. (d) Add a UFO warble, two alternating sine tones, while ufo is non-null. Start it at spawn (:460-469). Stop it at :499, at :600, and on pause and gameOver. (e) Add a new-best fanfare when isNewBest is true. Sustained voices use the bus's startLoop/stopLoop helper, so pause and unmount always silence them.

blitz-bomber canvas 15 h

Prerequisite fixes

  • Judder and half-speed class defect. The loop effect (Game.tsx:461-496) re-creates every frame and skips every other update. Measured live on hankshits.com on 2026-09-27 (rAF 121/s): the plane moved on 60 of 121 frames, at 89 px/s. Move the loop to the shared useGameLoop, and apply SIM_TIME_SCALE=0.5 in store.ts:179 so the speed is unchanged for kids.
  • Window strobe. drawBuildings rolls Math.random() for every window on every frame (Game.tsx:250-253), so windows flicker at 60 Hz. The result is visual noise, bitrate spikes and a mushy clip. Make the pattern deterministic with a hash of (building.id, row index counted from the ground, column, level). Rows must be anchored to the ground, not the roof, so the pattern survives the building shrinking by 35 px segments.
  • Final-frame points are lost. update calls get().crash()/get().land() (store.ts:287, :297, :304-306) before committing the frame's newScore and newBuildings, so the last building's +60 points are dropped when the landing triggers on the same frame. Commit the frame's state (a set with score and buildings) before calling land or crash.
  • Add real SFX and a findable 🔊 control, as described in the audio field.
  • Accidental restart. A tap, Space or Enter on the crashed or landed card instantly resets or advances (Game.tsx:499-509, :545-550). Ignore e.repeat, and add a 600 ms lockout after the transition, so the card and the kit's share chip stay reachable.
  • Hi-res backing store (S=1.28) for crisp HUD and card text.

Registration

File apps/web/src/games/blitz-bomber/Game.tsx. Insert the hook after the forceSync effect (after :80): useClipSource('blitz-bomber', { canvas: () => canvasRef.current /* ref :41, <canvas> :576-587 */, phase: () => ({ready:'menu', playing:'playing', paused:'paused', landed:'interstitial', crashed:'result'} as const)[useBlitzBomberStore.getState().gameState], layout: { width: 1024, height: 768 }, fps: 60, hud: () => { const s = useBlitzBomberStore.getState(); return { score: s.score, best: s.progress.highScore, level: s.level }; } }). Lifecycle facts: - The canvas is always mounted. There are no remounts. - The GameShell restart is reset (BlitzBomberGameShell.tsx:33). reset emits {type:'runAbort'} and a kit cut. - nextLevel (store.ts:386-404) continues the same run: the score carries over, so it emits no runStart. Backing store: - Set the width/height attributes (:578-579) to 1024x768 (S=1.28). Keep the CSS size (:583-584). - Call ctx.setTransform(S,0,0,S,0,0) as the first line of render (:414-417). Loop: - Replace the rAF effect (:461-496) with the shared useGameLoop. It reads state through getState() and calls the latest render through a ref. - Why: render changes identity on every store update, so the effect tears down every frame, and the cleanup's lastTimeRef=0 (:494) forces the 'skip first frame' branch (:470-475). Only every other frame updates. - Keep the feel kids know by multiplying normalizedDelta by SIM_TIME_SCALE=0.5 in update (store.ts:179). Measured on prod today: the plane moves at 89 px/s against a designed 3 px/frame, about 180 px/s. Flag this choice to Jack: 1.0 gives the designed speed.

What the compositor paints

No overlay painter is needed. The canvas paints its own HUD: 'Score:', 'Level n', 'Best:' (drawHUD :313-337). It also paints the CRASHED card with 'NEW HIGH SCORE!' (:339-371) and the LANDED card with 'Level n Complete!' and 'NEW HIGH SCORE!' (:373-411). Clip layout

Landscape 4:3, 1024x768 (786,432 px; both sides are multiples of 16). - Source: the 1.28x backing store, drawn 1:1. - Smoothing: enabled (vector art). - Editor 'story' variant: 720x1280 with the game at 720x540 centered. The bands use the sky gradient end colors #87CEEB above and #E0F6FF below (COLORS.SKY_TOP/SKY_BOTTOM, constants.ts). - Frame rate: 60 fps after the loop fix. Today the plane steps at half the display rate, which judders at any clip frame rate.

Brag events

All events come from the store, lib/store.ts, through createRunBrag('blitz-bomber'). - runStart: in startGame (:115-132), call brag.start(progress.highScore). - newHighScore (highlight, once per run, prevBest > 0 only): in update, after scoring (:242-247), call brag.score(newScore) just before the commit at :322. The kit ring timestamp then lands on the bomb hit that broke the record. - levelClear (highlight): in land() (:351-370), emit {type:'levelClear', level: state.level, score: finalScore}. The card flag becomes finalScore > bestAtRunStart. Today it compares against progress.highScore, which the previous landing in the same run has already bumped (:362). That is harmless on the landed card but wrong on the crash that follows. - 'cityCleared' marker: on the first update where allFlat (:293) becomes true. - runEnd: in crash() (:334-349), call const isNewBest = brag.end(state.score) and set isNewHighScore = isNewBest. - reset (:372-384): emit runAbort. - The terminal transitions must commit the frame first (see prerequisites). Otherwise brag.score misses points scored on the final frame.

Audio

The game is silent today. progress.settings.soundEnabled exists (store.ts:32, :48, setSoundEnabled :420-431), but there is no audio code and no control for it (#24i). Add games/blitz-bomber/lib/sounds.ts on the bus, gameAudio.out('blitz-bomber'): (1) Propeller engine drone, a sustained voice. Use a low-passed pulse around 55-70 Hz with a slight propeller-rate wobble, not a naked sawtooth (lessons.md), at gain about 0.04. Pitch rises about 3% per pass, tracking passCount (store.ts:191/195). It starts in startGame and nextLevel, and stops in crash, land, pauseGame, reset and on unmount. (2) Bomb whistle: a descending sine glide per bomb, started in dropBomb (:146-172) and stopped at impact (:219-220, :258). Cap: 3 concurrent. (3) Impact boom: a noise burst plus a 60 Hz thump on each segment hit (:237-242). (4) Building-collapse rumble plus a 'ding' when newHeight hits 0 (:245-248). (5) Crash explosion, with the drone pitching down, in crash(). (6) Landing fanfare (C-E-G-C) in land(). (7) New-best sparkle when isNewBest is true. Add a 🔊 toggle button (at least 44 px) to the stats row (Game.tsx:626-631), wired to setSoundEnabled and to gameAudio.setSpeakerMuted('blitz-bomber', !enabled). Every field a kid can set needs a findable control. Voices always feed the record tap.

bomberman canvas 15.5 h

Prerequisite fixes

  • Keyboard movement is dead on desktop. The loop effect (Game.tsx:288-318) is re-created every frame, which resets the local moveTimer (:289) before it can reach MOVE_RATE/speed (:302). Verified live on prod on 2026-09-27: holding ArrowRight or ArrowDown for 0.7-1.2 s never moved the player off tile (1,1), and that tile's neighbors are in the spawn safe zone (store.ts:175). Move to the shared useGameLoop with moveTimer in a ref.
  • highScore is lost on level wins. The movePlayer exit branch (store.ts:513-523) sets 'won' without updating progress.highScore. Centralize both win paths in winLevel().
  • The new-high-score check is wrong. Game.tsx:431 uses score === progress.highScore, which also shows on ties. Replace it with a store flag computed against the runStart snapshot.
  • The win jingle is mistimed (store.ts:405). Move it to the win moment.
  • Per-frame progress churn. update() writes progress.lastModified: Date.now() every frame (store.ts:793-796), and the persist middleware writes storage on every set (see groupNotes). Bump lastModified only when a progress counter actually changed.
  • update() mutates enemy objects that the previous state shares. It shallow-copies the array (store.ts:571) and then writes enemy.alive, x, y and lastMove (:677, :692, :724-725). Copy them with state.enemies.map(e => ({...e})). This is a one-line fix and keeps result-freeze snapshots and any later replay honest.
  • Hi-res backing store (S=1.5) for a crisp board and emoji in the clip.

dino-runner canvas 13 h

Prerequisite fixes

  • Half-rate update class defect. The loop effect (Game.tsx:435-470) re-creates every frame, so the simulation updates every other frame at half the designed speed (verified live on 2026-09-27). Move to the shared useGameLoop and apply SIM_TIME_SCALE=0.5 so the difficulty is unchanged.
  • Uncapped delta. update normalizes delta with no cap (store.ts:208), so a clip spanning a tab switch contains a teleport or instant-death frame. useGameLoop clamps dt to 50 ms, and the kit cuts on visibility change.
  • The new-high-score flag is computed in render (Game.tsx:363) after progress.highScore was maxed (store.ts:185). Move it to a store flag against the runStart snapshot.
  • Accidental restart. A tap or Space on the game-over canvas resets instantly (Game.tsx:473-481, :491-493). A kid mid jump-tap never sees the result or the share chip. Ignore e.repeat and add a 600 ms lockout.
  • Real SFX plus a findable 🔊 control for the existing soundEnabled field.
  • Hi-res backing store (S=1.6), and drop imageRendering:'pixelated'.

flappy-bird canvas 8.5 h

Prerequisite fixes

  • Restart grace. TIMING.RESTART_DELAY = 500 ms is defined (constants.ts:52-55) but never used. A pointerdown on the canvas at game over resets at once (Game.tsx:338-344; keys at 354-357). A kid who is still tapping at the moment of death skips the Game Over / NEW HIGH SCORE card (drawGameOver 188-256), and the brag clip loses its end card. Store gameOverAt in endGame and ignore input until RESTART_DELAY has passed.
  • Hi-DPI backing store. Set width/height to CANVAS_WIDTH*2 x CANVAS_HEIGHT*2 (Game.tsx:377-378) and call ctx.setTransform(2,0,0,2,0,0) as the first line of render (Game.tsx:261). The CSS size does not change. The live game gets sharper on phones, and the clip gets a native 640x960 source.
  • Add the session field bestAtRunStart to the store. It gives exact new-best and passed-best edges and stays correct when a cloud sync lands mid-run.
  • Add real sound effects through the shared bus, gated by the shared sound toggle. The game is silent today.

Registration

One canvas for the whole mount (Game.tsx:375-388). No remount: the header restart calls store.reset (games/flappy-bird/GameShell.tsx:9) and does not change a key. Add the hook right after the forceSync effect (after Game.tsx:56): useClipSource({ gameId: 'flappy-bird', canvasRef, layout: 'portrait', getHud: getFlappyHud, paintHud: paintFlappyBands }). The painters go in a new games/flappy-bird/lib/clipOverlay.ts. The store emits the run lifecycle through the module-level clipEvents, which needs no React: startGame (store.ts:101-115) calls clipEvents.runStart('flappy-bird') after its set. endGame (store.ts:225-247) emits the marks and then clipEvents.runEnd('flappy-bird'). reset (store.ts:249-262) calls clipEvents.phase('flappy-bird','menu'). The game has no pause (canPause={false}, GameShell.tsx:9); the kit handles a hidden tab through visibilitychange. The loop repaints every frame in every state (Game.tsx:304-322), so the kit never has to hold a frame for this game.

What the compositor paints

The game frame needs nothing added. The live score (drawScore, Game.tsx:170-186) and the full Game Over card (Score, Best, NEW HIGH SCORE!, the medal disc; drawGameOver 188-256) are painted in the canvas every frame. paintFlappyBands draws bands only. Top band: the kit title chip ('🐦 Flappy Bird' plus the leaderboard handle). Bottom band: 'Best {hud.best}', next-medal pips at the MEDALS thresholds 10/20/30/40 (constants.ts:58-63) in the game's own medal colors (#CD7F32, #C0C0C0, #FFD700, #E5E4E2, Game.tsx:232-237), and the brand. getHud reads useFlappyStore.getState() once per compositor frame and returns { score, best: bestAtRunStart, medal: getMedal(score) }. The band background is the page gradient the kid sees (from-sky-400 #38bdf8 to sky-600 #0284c7, Game.tsx:365). Draw the game rect with 8 px corners to match the canvas's rounded-lg chrome. No end card is needed. If a restart cuts the post-roll short, the kit's minimum hold extends the last Game Over frame to at least 1.5 s.

Clip layout

Portrait 720x1280 at 30 fps. After the hi-DPI prerequisite the backing store is 640x960 (2x of 320x480). It is drawn 1:1 with no scaling at x=40, y=160, with 160 px bands top and bottom (set imageSmoothingEnabled=false; nothing is resampled). The fallback without the hi-DPI fix is a 2x nearest-neighbour upscale of 320x480 (pixelated: true): crisp, but the Arial text and ellipses look jagged. The hi-DPI fix is the 10/10 path. It also sharpens the live game on 2-3x phones, where the 1x canvas is CSS-upscaled up to 2x today (Game.tsx:59-72, 384-387).

Brag events

Add a session field bestAtRunStart (not persisted). startGame sets it to progress.highScore (store.ts:103). setProgress (store.ts:265) sets it to max(bestAtRunStart, data.highScore) when a cloud sync lands mid-run. (1) newHighScore, featured. In endGame, use the existing pre-write flag isNewHighScore = score > progress.highScore. It is computed at line 228, before the write at line 235. Also require score > 0. Emit clipEvents.mark('flappy-bird', { kind: 'newHighScore', title: 'New best: {score} pipes!', priority: 'featured', preMs: the whole run capped at 60000, postMs: 2500 }). A tie never fires because the compare is strict. (2) passedBest, standard, rising edge, once per run. In update's scoring map (store.ts:172-180), fire when state.score <= bestAtRunStart, newScore > bestAtRunStart, and bestAtRunStart > 0. A session flag passedBestFired, reset in startGame, stops repeats. (3) milestone. When newScore crosses 10/20/30/40 (MEDALS), emit 'Bronze/Silver/Gold/Platinum medal!'. Bronze and silver are standard. Gold and platinum are featured, because they are the brag the game itself celebrates (Game.tsx:229-248). An update can add at most one pipe, so each crossing check is prev < T <= new. Both collision exits (store.ts:191 and 206) go through endGame, so every run has exactly one end event.

Audio

Silent today: a grep of games/flappy-bird finds no AudioContext, no Audio element, and no oscillator. Add lib/sounds.ts on the shared bus. Each chain ends in getGameAudio().input, never in ctx.destination, and every call is a no-op when the bus returns null (SSR or jsdom). The sounds and their call sites: - flap: triangle chirp 420->720 Hz, 70 ms, gain 0.12. Call it in flap() (store.ts:117-128), after the playing guard. - score: sine 880+1320 Hz, 90 ms. Call it in update when newScore > state.score (store.ts:172-180). - hit: 60 ms noise burst plus a 120 Hz thud. Call it in both collision branches (store.ts:190-193 and 203-208), before endGame. - die: square 600->120 Hz, 350 ms. Call it in endGame. - medal: C5-E5-G5 arpeggio when the final medal is new or better. - newBest: C5-E5-G5-C6-E6 fanfare in 60 ms steps. Call it in endGame when isNewHighScore is true. The game has no settings UI, so it is gated by the shared global sound preference. The shared SoundToggle on GameStartOverlay puts the control on this game's start card.

Replay log and renderer

n/a. This is live canvas capture. The seeded-PRNG, fixed-step replay from the feasibility map is not needed for clips.

hextris canvas 10 h

Prerequisite fixes

  • Touch double input, verified on prod: in Playwright iPhone 13 emulation, ONE tap on the right half moved targetRotation from 0 to 2.094 rad, two 60° turns (/tmp/hh-clips/g7/input.out). Cause: the canvas has both onClick (Game.tsx:427) and onTouchStart (428). React 19.2.3 registers touchstart as a passive root listener (node_modules/.pnpm/react-dom@19.2.3_react@19.2.3/node_modules/react-dom/cjs/react-dom-client.development.js:19251-19262), so e.preventDefault() at line 332 is ignored and the W3C Touch Events §9 compatibility click fires as well. At game over the same tap restarts (339), and then the compat click rotates the new run. Fix: one onPointerDown handler, the same pattern flappy uses (flappy Game.tsx:379-382).
  • New-high-score UI. Add isNewHighScore to the state, computed in gameOver before store.ts:294. Draw 'NEW HIGH SCORE!' in the canvas game-over card (Game.tsx:163-178) and show it in the DOM stats bar (407-413). Today the kid is never told about a new best.
  • Restart grace. Ignore restart input for 600 ms after game over (tap at 338-341, Space/Enter at 293-297), so the end card survives in the clip.
  • Frame-rate independence (stability, not a clip blocker). The falling block (store.ts:367-371) and the particles (492-500) move a fixed amount per update, not per deltaTime, and the loop runs once per display frame. On a 120 Hz Android Chrome phone, blocks fall twice as fast as on 60 Hz. Rotation at 344 is already dt-scaled. Multiply those steps by deltaTime.
  • Hi-DPI backing 2x. Double the canvas width/height attributes (Game.tsx:425-426) and call ctx.setTransform(2,0,0,2,0,0) at the top of render (Game.tsx:37-41).
  • Route audio through the shared bus (a two-line migration) and add the chain and new-best sounds.

Registration

One canvas for the whole mount (Game.tsx:423-435). The header restart calls store.startGame (HextrisGameShell.tsx:32), so nothing remounts. Add the hook after canvasRef (Game.tsx:210), next to useCanvasRenderer: useClipSource({ gameId: 'hextris', canvasRef, layout: 'portrait', getHud: getHextrisHud, paintHud: paintHextrisBands }). The canvas repaints every frame only while status is 'playing' (loop Game.tsx:236-257). In every other status it paints once per status change (260-262). The kit redraws the last bitmap on each compositor tick, so the idle, paused and game-over screens still produce frames. The store emits the lifecycle: - startGame (store.ts:258-270) calls clipEvents.runStart('hextris'). - pauseGame and resumeGame (272-284) call clipEvents.phase('hextris', 'paused' or 'playing'). This covers both the shell pause (HextrisGameShell.tsx:33-34) and the in-game 'II' button (Game.tsx:447-452). That button calls store.pauseGame directly and never opens the shell menu, so the kit must not rely on the shell's isPaused. - gameOver (286-298) emits the marks and then runEnd.

What the compositor paints

In the game frame, the canvas already paints the score (Game.tsx:157-160), the GAME OVER card with the final score (163-178), and PAUSED (188-200). The game never shows a new high score anywhere. Colors: BLOCK_COLORS (constants.ts:31-38), text #f8fafc (COLORS.SCORE_TEXT), background #0f172a (COLORS.BACKGROUND, constants.ts:41, the same slate-900 as the page). getHud reads useHextrisStore.getState() and returns { score, best: bestAtRunStart, chain: chainCount, status }.

Brag events

startGame (store.ts:258) sets bestAtRunStart = progress.highScore. setProgress (store.ts:520) raises it to the incoming cloud best when that is higher. (1) newHighScore, featured. In gameOver (store.ts:286), compute isNewHighScore = state.score > state.progress.highScore before the write at line 294, and store the flag (prerequisite). No mid-run code writes progress.highScore (lines 477-482 touch only the totals and longestChain), so this compare is exact. Mark: { kind: 'newHighScore', title: 'New high score: {score}!' }. (2) combo (chain), standard. The only increment is chainCount++ at store.ts:441. Emit 'Chain x{n}!' each time the new chainCount is 3 or more. Each increment is a new landing, so every level from 3 upward is its own rising edge. Make it featured ('Longest chain ever!') when chainCount >= 3 and it beats progress.longestChain as read before line 480. The chain reset at 484 needs no event. (3) bigMoment, standard. A match of 5 or more blocks (the POINTS.MATCH_5_PLUS branch, store.ts:437) emits 'Mega match!'. (4) passedBest, standard, rising edge. Fire once when the score first goes above bestAtRunStart (and bestAtRunStart > 0), inside update's final set (502-516).

hill-climb canvas 28 h

Prerequisite fixes

  • Canvas sizing. The canvas is sized to window.innerWidth x innerHeight (Game.tsx:1012-1013, 1132-1136) inside a container that is 100vh minus the header (1186). Its bottom 48-56 px therefore render below the fold, and the page scrolls. Measured on prod (/tmp/hh-clips/g7/layout.out): on desktop the canvas sits at top 56 and is 1440x900 in a 900 px viewport (scrollHeight 956); in iPhone 13 landscape it sits at top 48 and is 750x342 in a 342 px viewport. Size the canvas from its container (a ResizeObserver on the root div). The camera then centers the truck in the visible area, and the clip source equals what the kid saw.
  • Hi-DPI. Make the backing store the container size x min(devicePixelRatio, 2) and call ctx.setTransform(s,0,0,s,0,0) at the top of each frame (render, Game.tsx:703). Replace the 21 canvas.width/canvas.height reads in the render path (sky 713-796, camera 831, snowfall and embers 937-956) with logical width/height refs. Today a DPR-3 phone renders at 1/3 resolution (backing measured 750x342). That is blurry live and cannot feed a sharp 720p clip.
  • The combo never expires. incrementCombo sets comboTimer to 2 (store.ts:341-346), but nothing counts it down, and resetCombo (348-350) is never called. After the first flip, 'xN COMBO!' stays on the HUD for the whole run, and the coin multiplier (store.ts:321) only grows. Count comboTimer down by dt in gameLoop and call resetCombo when it reaches 0.
  • Crash linger (feel and clip). On the head hit (handleCollision, Game.tsx:516-519), record crashAt, stop applying torque, keep physics and rendering running for 1.0 s, then call endRun('head'). Today the game freezes on the impact frame, so both the kid and the clip miss the tumble. The kit's end card then plays over a frame that actually shows the crash. This changes how the game feels, so show Hank.
  • Touch zones must ignore interactive targets. The window touchstart handler (hooks/useControls.ts:194-212) assigns every touch to gas or brake by screen half. That includes touches on the pause button (GameUI.tsx:38) and on any clip button the kit adds. Skip a touch when target.closest('button, a, [role=button], [data-no-drive]') matches.
  • GameOverScreen tie bug. isNewRecord (GameOverScreen.tsx:28) is true on a tie; use lastRunNewBest from endRun.
  • Add real sound effects and engine audio (the sound toggle is dead today). Either add a real music bed or remove the dead Music toggle.

Registration

The canvas element changes identity. Every header restart remounts the whole game through key={gameKey} (hill-climb GameShell.tsx:16, 32), and the Garage replaces the tree (Game.tsx:1177-1179), so coming back mounts a new canvas. Register in HillClimbGame after canvasRef (Game.tsx:76): useClipSource({ gameId: 'hill-climb', canvasRef, active: isPlaying || isGameOver, layout: 'auto', fit: 'width', getHud: getHillHud, paintHud: paintHillHud, endCard: paintHillEndCard }). This relies on three kit contracts: - Registrations are keyed by gameId, so an unmount plus mount pair (a restart) continues the same ring buffer instead of starting a new capture session. - The ref is re-read on every tick. - While the start overlay is up, the canvas exists but is not painted; that is why active is false then. The store emits the lifecycle: - startRun (store.ts:209-230, also reached through restartRun 254-256) emits runStart and snapshots bestAtRunStart and stageBestAtRunStart. - endRun (232-251) emits the marks and then runEnd. - pauseGame and resumeGame (266-272) emit phase changes. Pause comes from the game's own button (GameUI.tsx:36-46) and usePauseKeyboard; the shell pause is disabled (GameShell.tsx:24-25). At game over, the lifecycle cleanup (Game.tsx:1113-1123) cancels the rAF and clears the engine, so the canvas freezes on the crash frame. The kit holds that frame and paints the end card over it.

What the compositor paints

The whole HUD and the result screen are DOM (GameUI.tsx:34-149, GameOverScreen.tsx:31-124), so the painter rebuilds them. paintHillHud mirrors GameUI's layout, scaled to the output frame: - Top-left stack: a distance card '{floor(distance)}m' in bold with 'Best: {bestAtRunStart}m' (background rgba(0,0,0,0.5), radius 8). Coins '🪙 +{sessionCoins}' (text-yellow-400, #facc15). '🔄 {flips} flips' when above 0. A combo pill 'x{combo} COMBO!' (bg-purple-600/80 = rgba(147,51,234,0.8)) when combo > 0. - Top-right: a fuel bar 192x16 logical. It is green-500 #22c55e above 50%, yellow-500 #eab308 below that, and red-500 #ef4444 under FUEL.LOW_FUEL_THRESHOLD 20 (constants.ts:101). A nitro bar (cyan-500 #06b6d4; cyan-400 #22d3ee plus 'BOOST!' while active). The speed as '{speed} km/h'. - Center: a pulsing 'LOW FUEL!' pill (red-600/90) when fuel is low. - Skip the pause button, the total-coins wallet and the touch-zone hints. getHud returns { distance, bestAtRunStart, sessionCoins, flips, combo, fuel, maxFuel: getVehicleStats(currentVehicleId).maxFuel, nitro, nitroActive, speed, stageName, vehicleName }. Most fields come from useHillClimbStore.getState(). Speed comes from a ref set next to setSpeed (Game.tsx:669). nitroActive comes from controlsRef plus nitroRef. paintHillEndCard: 3 s over the held crash frame, dimmed to 60%. It mirrors GameOverScreen: - '💀 CRASHED!' (#ef4444) with 'Your head hit the ground!', or '⛽ OUT OF FUEL!' (#f97316). - A gold 'NEW RECORD!' banner, only when distance > bestAtRunStart. - Rows for Distance, Coins Earned, Flips and Air Time. - '{vehicleName} on {stageName}'.

Clip layout

'auto' picks the orientation from the source aspect. Landscape phones and desktops give 1280x720. The game shows OrientationWarning in portrait (Game.tsx:1210); a kid who taps 'continue anyway' in portrait gets 720x1280. Fit-width: scale = 1280 / visible source width. - If the scaled height is above 720, crop vertically, centered on the truck's screen y (getHud supplies it). - If it is shorter, letterbox and draw the HUD into the bars. Ultra-wide phones hit this case: iPhone 13 landscape measured 750x342 CSS, an aspect of about 2.55 after the header fix. Do NOT center-crop to 16:9 on phones. The camera puts the look-ahead point at the center (CAMERA.LOOK_AHEAD 200 px, constants.ts:526), so a 16:9 crop of a 750-wide view would leave the truck about 60 px from the left edge. Use smooth scaling; this is vector art.

Brag events

Snapshot at startRun: bestAtRunStart = bestDistance, stageBestAtRunStart = bestDistancePerStage[currentStageId] || 0, unlockedAtRunStart = unlockedStages. Raise them to incoming cloud data in setProgress (store.ts:527-546). (1) newHighScore, featured. endRun (store.ts:232) computes isNewBest = state.distance > state.bestDistance BEFORE the set at 241 and emits 'New record: {m}m!'. Store the flag as lastRunNewBest and make GameOverScreen use it; its isNewRecord (GameOverScreen.tsx:28) reads after the write and is true on a tie. (2) unlock, featured. Any stage in getUnlockedStagesForDistance(...) (250) that is not in unlockedAtRunStart emits 'Unlocked {stage}!'. (3) Stage best, standard. When (1) did not fire and distance > stageBestAtRunStart, emit 'Best on {stage}!'. (4) passedBest, standard, rising edge in updateDistance (258-260): prev <= bestAtRunStart < new, with bestAtRunStart > 0. (5) flip, standard, in addFlip (Game.tsx:636-644). 'Double flip!' is featured when two flips complete in one airborne span. Count flips per span in a ref that resets at takeoff (620-625). (6) Big air, standard. A landing after airborneTimeRef > 1.5 s (647-661) emits 'Big air {t}s!'. (7) Clutch, standard. collectFuel (store.ts:293-297, reached through Game.tsx:523-534) while fuel is under 10% emits 'Saved by fuel!'.

Audio

Silent today. games/hill-climb has no audio code, while the soundEnabled and musicEnabled toggles (SettingsMenu.tsx:61-101, store.ts:489-495) control nothing. Add lib/sounds.ts on the shared bus, gated by soundEnabled: - Engine loop: start it at run start; stop it at endRun, on pause and on unmount. Two detuned sawtooths with a fundamental of 55-165 Hz, mapped from rear-wheel angular speed and gas, run through a lowpass whose cutoff follows the throttle. Update them each frame from gameLoop (Game.tsx:559-671) with setTargetAtTime. - Nitro: a bandpass noise loop at 1.5 kHz while nitroActive. - Coin: an 880/1320 Hz ding (Game.tsx:538-551). - Fuel: a rising glug arpeggio (523-535). - Flip: a whoosh plus a ding (636-644). - Landing thud, with gain set by the airtime (647-661). - Crash: a noise burst plus a 70 Hz clang (511-520). - Out of fuel: the engine pitch dives to 30 Hz over 600 ms (consumeFuel, store.ts:282-291). - Low fuel: an 880 Hz beep every 1 s under 20%. - New record: a fanfare at endRun. musicEnabled gets a real music bed: a small step sequencer (bass plus lead, 8 bars, with a per-stage key and tempo from STAGES) at gain 0.08. If Hank does not want music, delete the Music toggle instead. The reference is the engine synth already in games/monster-truck/lib/sounds.ts (startEngine, line 319).

platformer canvas 13 h

Prerequisite fixes

  • Honest timer, verified on prod (/tmp/hh-clips/g7/loops.out): holding right through Level 1 took 11,534 ms of wall time, but the stored bestTime was 5,862 ms (ratio 1.97). Cause: the loop effect (Game.tsx:607-642) depends on render, and render's identity changes on every store update (cameraX and the rest, 588-603). So the loop re-arms every frame and zeroes lastTimeRef (640). Measured: 57.9 cancelAnimationFrame calls per second at 116.6 fps. update() and 'timeElapsed = state.timeElapsed + delta' (store.ts:309) therefore run only on every other frame. The HUD timer, NEW BEST TIME! and the stored best times all show half the real time, and a clip exposes that at once. Fix without changing the tuned movement feel: keep the physics as is and compute timeElapsed from the wall clock. Set runStartedAt = performance.now() in startGame, accumulate pausedAccumMs in pauseGame/resumeGame, and compute timeElapsed = now - runStartedAt - pausedAccumMs in update. GameShell pauseOnBlur already covers a hidden tab. Migrate old records x2: - Persist version 1 with a migrate() that doubles levels[*].bestTime (store.ts:663-666). - Add timerVersion: 2 to PlatformerProgress and to platformerSchema (progress-schemas.ts:338-351). That schema is a non-strict z.object, which would otherwise strip the field. - In setProgress (store.ts:661), double the bestTimes when incoming cloud data lacks timerVersion. The leaderboard and the profile stats do not read bestTime (leaderboard-extractors.ts:282-292, gameStatExtractor.ts:252-264), so nothing else moves.
  • End-state input grace of 700 ms (handleTap 645-655, Space 671-675, touchstart 735-737), so a finishing tap cannot skip the LEVEL COMPLETE or Oops card.

space-invaders canvas 14 h

Prerequisite fixes

  • Move MobileControls (Game.tsx:755-894), SettingsPanel (899-943) and StatsDisplay (948-978) to module scope. They are declared inside SpaceInvadersGame's body, so every store update creates a new component type and React remounts them. During play the store updates every frame. The react.dev 'Preserving and Resetting State' pitfall says never to nest component definitions. Verified on prod in Playwright iPhone 13 emulation (/tmp/hh-clips/g7/si.out): the Move-left DOM node was replaced within 100 ms. Holding the button for 1 s moved playerX from 205 to 200, one 5 px step, where the 50 ms repeat (line 767) should give about 100 px. Each remount's cleanup (810-816) clears the interval. Phone players cannot hold to move or to auto-fire, so phones produce no brag clips.
  • The canvas has onClick (998) and onTouchStart (999-1004), the same passive-touchstart double dispatch verified on hextris. At game over one tap runs reset (691), and then the compat click runs startGame (687), which skips the start card and the age picker. Use one onPointerDown, and add a 700 ms grace after gameOver and waveComplete.
  • Audio unlock. The AudioContext created at import time measured 'suspended' at load and turned 'running' only after the first gesture, because Chromium auto-resumes a context when a source starts after user activation (/tmp/hh-clips/g7/loops.out). WebKit gives no such guarantee. The shared bus calls resume() inside the first pointerdown/keydown (MDN Autoplay guide).
  • Wire the dead sounds, move the wave-clear sound to the moment of the clear, and keep rejected shots silent.
  • Hi-DPI backing 2x. Double the canvas attributes (Game.tsx:996-997) and call setTransform at the top of render (568).
  • Cosmetic: UI.SMALL_FONT requests 'Press Start 2P' (constants.ts:327), but the font is never loaded (app/layout.tsx:2 loads only Nunito through next/font), so the HUD falls back to generic monospace. Load it with next/font/google (Press_Start_2P), scoped to this island, or drop it.

Phase 3: 3D and iframe games

monster-truck r3f 41 h

Prerequisite fixes

  • Flip detector counts false flips. Vehicle.tsx:138-139 accumulates Math.abs(euler.x - last.x). three's XYZ Euler x wraps at ±π, so a half flip onto the roof adds about 3π and is credited as a FLIP with +50 coins. Use a wrap-safe delta (atan2(sin d, cos d)), or integrate the local lateral angular velocity. Without this, 'DOUBLE FLIP!' brag clips lie.
  • Pause is cosmetic. <Physics> at Game.tsx:74 has no paused prop and Vehicle's useFrame ignores isPaused, so the truck drifts while paused and a clip jump-cuts on resume. Add paused={isPaused || showGarage || !hasStarted} and early-return the Vehicle useFrame when paused.
  • Audio: move the 11 ctx.destination sites (sounds.ts:52-342) onto the shared bus channels. Wire setEnabled so the ungated sounds (Vehicle.tsx:145, 156, 321) respect the toggle. Replace the broken first-click resume (Game.tsx:136-149) with the kit's in-gesture unlock.
  • Triple horn: Game.tsx:124-126 + MobileControls.tsx:86-91 + Vehicle.tsx:316-327 all play the horn for one press. Make the Vehicle rising edge the single owner, and fold in issue #18i (pointer events instead of passive touch preventDefault).
  • Add the missing engine sound. startEngine (sounds.ts:319) is never called; drive it from SpeedTracker.
  • Make the dead music toggle real (GameUI.tsx:323-328): add a procedural music loop on the music channel.
  • Celebrations: BonusPopup (GameUI.tsx:255-277) is exported but never rendered, so flips, big air and challenge completions show nothing to the player. Render a DOM toast for the same events the kit gets, restyled to a solid fill (its current yellow-to-orange gradient plus animate-bounce is an AI-slop tell).
  • Persisted records {bestAirtimeMs, mostFlipsOneJump}: add them to MonsterTruckProgress (store.ts:10-32), getProgress/setProgress/partialize, and monsterTruckSchema. That schema is .strict() (lib/progress-schemas.ts:252-293), so the schema must land in the same PR, or every monster-truck cloud save is rejected. Use .optional() for old saves. Add a 'Best Air' stat in shared/lib/gameStatExtractor.ts:57-67.
  • hasStarted: move it from React state (Game.tsx:102) into the store as non-persisted session state.
  • One-line copy bug found while reading: the start overlay says 'Hold Shift for a speed boost' (Game.tsx:232), but Shift is BRAKE and Space is NOS (hooks/useControls.ts:84-90; GameUI.tsx:231 is correct).

Registration

WHERE: new island file games/monster-truck/components/ClipSource.tsx, rendered inside GameScene right after <SpeedTracker> at games/monster-truck/Game.tsx:91 (inside <Canvas>, outside <Physics>): `<MonsterTruckClipSource vehicleRef={vehicleRef} />`. It makes one kit call (proposed kit API, shared/clips/r3f.ts): useR3FClipSource({ appId: 'monster-truck', cropTop: 'shell-header', fit: 'auto', fps: 30, bitrateHint: 4_000_000, hud: () => monsterTruckHud(useGameStore.getState(), vehicleRef.current), idle: () => { const s = useGameStore.getState(); return !s.hasStarted || s.isPaused || s.showGarage || s.showChallenges; }, }) HOW THE KIT HOOK WORKS: it reads gl with useThree and subscribes addAfterEffect(ts => source.pushFrame(ts)). In R3F 9.6.1, after-effects run in the same rAF task, after every root's gl.render (node_modules/@react-three/fiber/dist/events-b389eeca.esm.js:16071-16100). That is the only safe read point for a preserveDrawingBuffer:false WebGL2 canvas. The WebGL spec clears the buffer after compositing (registry.khronos.org/webgl/specs/latest/1.0/, section 2.2). Do NOT use useFrame, because it runs BEFORE the render at :16058-16061. Do NOT use querySelector('canvas'). STATE MOVE: hasStarted is local React state today (Game.tsx:102). RESTART: GameShell (games/monster-truck/GameShell.tsx:13-16,27) bumps key={gameKey}. The whole MonsterTruckGame remounts, so the Canvas and gl are new. - The hook's effect cleanup unsubscribes the after-effect and drops the canvas ref. - The new Canvas registers the new gl. - The kit must hot-swap the source without resetting the replay ring, and force a keyframe at the swap. OTHER CASES: - WebGLGate fallback (Game.tsx:194): no Canvas means no source, so the kit shows 'clips not available on this device'. - There is no second canvas; Garage.tsx is DOM only. - OrientationWarning (Game.tsx:189) covers portrait phones. It has no shared visibility signal today. Publish it through a presence store modeled on shared/lib/startOverlayPresence.ts so the kit idles while the warning is up. - GameShell passes canPause={false} (GameShell.tsx:22), so the Clip control must live in the shell header right cluster, not in the shared PauseMenu.

What the compositor paints

FIT 'auto': - Cover (center-crop) when the cropped source aspect is within ±20% of 16:9. Example: desktop 1200x808 is 1.49, so crop top and bottom. - Otherwise contain, and use the bars as the HUD strip. Example: phone landscape 844x342 is 2.47, giving a 1280x518 frame with 101 px strips; the HUD goes in the bottom strip. - A resize or rotation only changes the source rect, never the encoder dims. RATE: 30 fps, 4 Mbps hint (foliage and terrain detail). This game is heavy (shadows, Rapier), so on iOS the default ring length should be 30 s to stay clear of Safari memory kills.

Brag events

All events go to the kit via a module-level emit (proposed: clips.emit({appId, type, priority, title, payload})). Priority 2 or higher auto-saves a highlight clip (pre-roll 8 s, post-roll 2 s). Lower priorities only drop a marker on the replay timeline. 1) TRICK LANDING (priority 2). Vehicle.tsx:149-157 is the landing branch (grounded after airborneTime > 0.5). - Add a per-jump counter flipsThisJump. Increment it where a flip is credited (Vehicle.tsx:142-147). Reset it at takeoff (:131-134) and after landing. - On landing, emit {type:'trick', flips, airtimeS} when flips >= 1 or airtimeS >= 2.0. - Title: flips 1 = 'FLIP!', 2 = 'DOUBLE FLIP!', 3+ = 'TRIPLE FLIP!', else 'BIG AIR x.x s'. - Rising edge is structural: the event fires exactly once per landing, because isAirborne goes false in the same branch (:158). 2) NEW RECORD (priority 3). Add persisted records {bestAirtimeMs, mostFlipsOneJump} with a store action recordJump(airtimeMs, flips). The action returns {airtimeRecord, flipRecord, prevAirtimeMs, prevFlips}. It compares against the value read in the SAME set() before writing, and only strictly-greater counts. A first-ever record needs airtime >= 1.5 s or flips >= 1, so the first tiny hop does not brag. Emit {type:'newHighScore', metric:'airtime'|'flips', value, previous, better:'higher'} after set(). Refresh-proof: the compare is against the persisted best. 3) CHALLENGE COMPLETE (priority 3). getChallengeCompletionUpdate (store.ts:267-281) already knows which challenges flip. Change it to also return newlyCompleted: Challenge[]. The five callers (addCoins 312-327, addAirtime 400-413, addFlip 415-428, addDestruction 430-443, collectStar 445-458) emit one event per newly completed challenge after their set(). Do NOT detect this with a store subscription on challenges[i].completed: cloud hydration (useAuthSync setState -> setProgress, store.ts:562-571) and persist rehydrate flip the same field and would fire bogus brags. 4) SMASH COMBO (priority 2). The Destructibles handlers (Destructibles.tsx:276-296) push performance.now() into a module ring. Three or more smashes inside 2.5 s emits {type:'combo', count} once, updated to the final count when the combo window closes. A car smash (:289-296) alone is a priority 1 marker. 5) MARKERS ONLY (priority 1): mystery box (Collectibles.tsx:288-294) and star pickup (:282-286). NOT EVENTS: truck unlock and upgrades (Garage.tsx:31-47). They happen in the DOM garage while the kit is idle.

SRCDOC PRELUDE: in games/four-wheeler-adventure/Game.tsx:72-76, change `setGameHtml(html)` to `setGameHtml(injectClipPrelude(html))`. Hank's file itself stays byte-identical, which honors the wrapper contract in Game.tsx:12-20. CSP allows it: a srcdoc document inherits the parent CSP, and script-src has 'unsafe-inline', which Hank's own inline script already relies on. WHAT THE PRELUDE DEFINES: (a) AUDIO BUS. `class HHAudioContext extends (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)`. Its constructor calls super(), creates `bus = this.createGain()`, connects bus to super.destination, then Object.defineProperty(this, 'destination', {value: bus}). Assign window.AudioContext = window.webkitAudioContext = HHAudioContext and publish window.__hhAudio = {ctx, bus}. Every one of Hank's five output sites connects to audioCtx.destination (index.html:1631, 1711, 5182, 5195, 6044), so the whole mix now flows through bus. (b) iOS AUTO-RESUME. On the first pointerdown, touchend or keydown, a capture listener on document calls ctx.resume() if the context is suspended. index.html never calls resume() (grep). The context is created by startAudio (:1705-1734) from the parent's programmatic playBtn.click() in onLoad (Game.tsx:54-57), which is not a gesture, so on WebKit the game is likely silent today (verify on a real iPhone). (c) PROBE. window.__hhProbe = () => { try { return {running, raceState: race.state, raceTime: race.time, winner: race.winner, raceCar, collected, total, money, skulls: mountedSkulls, rainbowTimer, mode, inSpace, onPlanet}; } catch { return null; } }. It works without eval because all classic scripts share one global lexical environment, so Hank's top-level let/const bindings resolve at call time. Declarations: index.html:747 race, 756 raceCar, 772 inSpace/onPlanet, 884 mode, 933 mountedSkulls, 977 rainbowTimer, 1042-1043 collected/total/money, 5398 running. (d) PANEL WATCH. REGISTRATION: in handleIframeLoad (Game.tsx:52-62), after playBtn.click(), run `disposeRef.current?.(); disposeRef.current = registerAdventureClipSource(iframeRef.current!, {restoreFocus: () => iframeRef.current?.contentWindow?.focus()})`. The adapter lives at games/four-wheeler-adventure/clip/source.ts. WHAT THE ADAPTER DOES: - It pushes frames from the IFRAME's own rAF: win.requestAnimationFrame(tick), re-armed each tick. It registers after Hank's loop (index.html:5399-5405, first armed at :6614), so it runs after draw() in the same frame. The HTML spec runs a child document's rAF AFTER its container's (html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html, update the rendering), so parent-rAF reads would lag one frame. A 2D canvas is readable at any time anyway. - Timestamps must be converted: tsParent = tsIframe + (win.performance.timeOrigin − performance.timeOrigin). Each document has its own time origin (w3c.github.io/hr-time). - Audio: kit.attachForeignTap(win.__hhAudio.bus, win) (see groupNotes). - Idle: !probe || !probe.running || win.__hhPanelOpen. - Dispose on the next onLoad and in a useEffect cleanup on unmount. RESTART: key={restartNonce} (Game.tsx:106) remounts the iframe. The prelude re-runs, onLoad fires, and the adapter re-registers. The old realm's context dies with its document, so the kit must drop that tap and zero-fill. FOCUS: after any kit dialog closes, call restoreFocus(). Keyboard players otherwise lose control, because Game.tsx:61 hands focus to the iframe. SHELL: the route (app/games/four-wheeler-adventure/page.tsx:33-40) passes appId and canPause={false}, so the Clip control goes in the header. An invariant test pins that the iframe has NO sandbox attribute, since every hook depends on same-origin access.
